--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Test 001: MOV between registers (32-bit, 16-bit, and 8-bit)
section .text
global _start
_start:
    ; 32-bit register to register
    mov eax, ebx
    mov ecx, edx
    mov esi, edi
    mov ebp, esp

    ; 16-bit register to register
    mov ax, bx
    mov cx, dx
    mov si, di
    mov bp, sp

    ; 8-bit register to register (low and high)
    mov al, bl
    mov cl, dl
    mov ah, bh
    mov ch, dh
    mov al, ah
    mov bl, ch
    ret

1. SOURCE CODE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Test 002: MOV register with all immediate formats (decimal, hex, binary, octal, negative)
section .text
global _start
_start:
    ; Decimal immediates
    mov eax, 100
    mov ebx, -42
    mov ecx, 255d

    ; Hexadecimal formats (0x and 'h' suffix)
    mov edx, 0x12345678
    mov esi, 0FFH
    mov edi, 0x7FFFFFFF

    ; Binary format ('b' suffix)
    mov al, 10101010b
    mov ah, 11110000b

    ; Octal format ('o' and 'q' suffix)
    mov cl, 77o
    mov ch, 177q

    ; Sized 16-bit immediates
    mov ax, 0x1234
    mov bx, -300
    ret

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. SOURCE CODE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Test 003: Memory addressing with [base] and [base + disp]
section .text
global _start
_start:
    ; Simple base register addressing
    mov eax, [ebx]
    mov ecx, [esi]
    mov edx, [edi]
    mov ebx, [ebp]
    mov esi, [esp]

    ; Base + positive displacement (disp8 and disp32)
    mov eax, [ebx + 4]
    mov ecx, [esi + 128]
    mov edx, [edi + 0x1000]

    ; Base + negative displacement
    mov eax, [ebp - 4]
    mov ebx, [ebp - 8]
    mov ecx, [esp + 16]
    ret

; Test 004: SIB memory addressing [base + index*scale + disp]
section .text
global _start
_start:
    ; Base + index (scale 1 implicit or explicit)
    mov eax, [ebx + ecx]
    mov edx, [esi + edi*1]

    ; Base + index with scale factors: 2, 4, 8
    mov eax, [ebx + edx*2]
    mov ecx, [esi + edi*4]
    mov edx, [ebp + eax*8]

    ; Base + index*scale + displacement
    mov eax, [ebx + ecx*4 + 16]
    mov ebx, [esi + edx*8 + 0x20]
    mov edx, [ebp + edi*2 - 8]
    mov ecx, [esp + eax*4 + 12]
    ret

Category      : VALID TEST CASE

1. SOURCE CODE

; Test 007: XCHG across all operand combinations
section .text
global _start
_start:
    ; Accumulator short form (90+r)
    xchg eax, ebx
    xchg ecx, eax
    xchg edx, eax
    xchg ax, bx
    xchg cx, ax

    ; General register to register
    xchg ebx, ecx
    xchg esi, edi
    xchg bl, cl
    xchg ah, bh

    ; Register with memory
    xchg eax, dword ptr [ebx]
    xchg ax, word ptr [ecx]
    xchg al, byte ptr [edx]
    xchg dword ptr [esi + 4], edi
    ret

================================================================================
TEST CODE RUN RESPONSE: test_012.asm
================================================================================
Test Number   : 012
Test Title    : Test 012: LOAD and STORE pseudo-instructions
Category      : VALID TEST CASE
Overall Result: PASS (Valid Assembly Assembled & Linked Successfully)
================================================================================

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. SOURCE CODE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Test 012: LOAD and STORE pseudo-instructions
section .text
global _start
_start:
    ; LOAD reg, reg  -> MOV reg, [reg]
    load eax, ebx
    load ecx, esi

    ; LOAD reg, [mem] -> MOV reg, [mem]
    load edx, [ebp - 4]
    load eax, [ebx + ecx*4]

    ; LOAD reg, imm -> MOV reg, imm
    load eax, 100
    load ebx, 0x500

    ; STORE reg, reg -> MOV [reg], reg
    store ebx, eax
    store edi, ecx

    ; STORE [mem], reg -> MOV [mem], reg
    store [ebp - 8], edx
    store [esi + 4], eax
    ret

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    ; 32-bit ADD

    add ecx, 10
    add edx, 0x12345678

    add dword ptr [esi], 5

    ; 16-bit ADD

    add cx, 20

    add word ptr [edi], 1

    ; 8-bit ADD

    add ah, 1

    add byte ptr [edi], 3
    ret

    cmp ah, 0

; Test 021: XOR bitwise logic and register clearing
section .text
global _start
_start:
    ; Clearing registers with XOR
    xor eax, eax
    xor ecx, ecx
    xor edx, edx
    xor ebx, ebx
    xor esi, esi
    xor edi, edi

    ; XOR with memory and immediate
    xor eax, [ebx + 4]
    xor [esi], edx
    xor dword ptr [edi], 0xAA55AA55

    ; 16-bit and 8-bit XOR
    xor ax, bx
    xor word ptr [ebx], 0xFFFF
    xor al, bl
    xor byte ptr [edx], 0x0F
    ret

    ; 32-bit INC / DEC (single-byte 40+r / 48+r opcodes)
    inc eax
    inc ecx
    inc edx
    inc ebx
    inc esp
    inc ebp
    inc esi
    inc edi

    dec edi

    dec ebp

    dec edx

    dec dx
    dec bx

    inc ah
    dec bl
    dec bh

    dec dword ptr [esi + 4]
    inc word ptr [ecx]
    dec byte ptr [edx]
    ret

; Test 027: IMUL (1-operand, 2-operand, and 3-operand forms)
section .text
global _start
_start:
    ; 1-operand IMUL (register only)
    imul ecx
    imul edx
    imul ax
    imul bx
    imul al
    imul bl

    ; 2-operand IMUL: r, r
    imul eax, ebx
    imul ecx, edx
    imul ax, bx

    ; 3-operand IMUL: r, r, imm (imm8 and imm32)
    imul eax, ebx, 5
    imul ebx, ecx, 1000
    imul ax, bx, 10

    ; 2-operand IMUL: r, mem
    imul eax, dword ptr [ebx]
    imul ecx, dword ptr [esi + 4]

    ret

; Test 028: SHL and SAL logical/arithmetic shift left
section .text
global _start
_start:
    ; SHL with 1 (short D1 opcode)
    shl eax, 1
    shl ebx, 1
    sal ecx, 1

    ; SHL / SAL with imm8 (2..31)
    shl eax, 2
    shl edx, 16
    sal esi, 31

    ; SHL / SAL with CL register count
    shl eax, cl
    sal ebx, cl

    ; Sized memory operands
    shl dword ptr [ebx], 1
    shl dword ptr [esi], 4
    shl dword ptr [edi], cl
    shl word ptr [ecx], 2
    shl byte ptr [edx], 1
    ret

================================================================================
TEST CODE RUN RESPONSE: test_060.asm

Test Number   : 060
Test Title    : Test 060: Comprehensive realistic pipeline program
Category      : VALID TEST CASE
Overall Result: PASS (Valid Assembly Assembled & Linked Successfully)
================================================================================

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. SOURCE CODE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Test 060: Comprehensive realistic pipeline program
global _start

section .data
msg:     db "Hello, x86-32!", 10
len equ  14

section .bss
res_buf: resb 32

section .text
_start:
    ; Function prologue
    push ebp
    mov ebp, esp

    ; Setup loop to copy msg into res_buf
    lea esi, msg
    lea edi, res_buf
    mov ecx, len

copy_loop:
    lodsb
    stosb
    loop copy_loop

    ; Write syscall: eax=4, ebx=1, ecx=res_buf, edx=len
    mov eax, 4
    mov ebx, 1
    lea ecx, res_buf
    mov edx, len
    int 0x80

    ; Exit syscall: eax=1, ebx=0
    leave
    mov eax, 1
    xor ebx, ebx
    int 0x80

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
